    <!-- 4 Угловых квадрата-активатора -->
    <!-- Левый верхний: Стрелка Влево -->

    <div class="hint-overlay">КЛИКАЙТЕ ПО ГРАНЯМ ПРАВОГО КУБА-ПУЛЬТА ИЛИ УГЛОВЫМ КВАДРАТАМ</div>

    <script>
        // ==========================================
        // 1. СИНТЕЗАТОР ЗВУКА & KARAOKE ENGINE (Web Audio API)
        // ==========================================
        class AudioKaraokeEngine {
            constructor() {
                this.ctx = null;
                this.isPlaying = false;
                this.currentLine = 0;
                this.currentWord = 0;
                this.timer = null;

                this.lyrics = [
                    { line: "Хрустальный куб стоит на острой грани", words: ["Хрустальный", "куб", "стоит", "на", "острой", "грани"] },
                    { line: "Бронза и золото сияют в квантовом тумане", words: ["Бронза", "и", "золото", "сияют", "в", "квантовом", "тумане"] },
                    { line: "Вращение плавно пробуждает звук", words: ["Вращение", "плавно", "пробуждает", "звук"] },
                    { line: "Симфония импульса и магия рук", words: ["Симфония", "импульса", "и", "магия", "рук"] }
                ];
            }

            init() {
                if (!this.ctx) {
                    const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
                    this.ctx = new AudioContext();
                }
            }

            playClickChome(freq = 440) {
                this.init();
                if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume();

                const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                const gain = this.ctx.createGain();

                osc.type = 'sine';
                osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, this.ctx.currentTime);
                osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(freq * 1.8, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.15);

                gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.2, this.ctx.currentTime);
                gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.15);

                osc.connect(gain);
                gain.connect(this.ctx.destination);

                osc.start();
                osc.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 0.15);
            }

            startKaraokeSequence() {
                this.init();
                if (this.isPlaying) return;
                this.isPlaying = true;
                this.renderLine();

                this.timer = setInterval(() => {
                    this.advanceWord();
                }, 450);
            }

            renderLine() {
                const lineData = this.lyrics[this.currentLine];
                const activeContainer = document.getElementById('line-active');
                activeContainer.innerHTML = '';

                lineData.words.forEach((word, idx) => {
                    const span = document.createElement('span');
                    span.className = 'karaoke-word';
                    span.id = `word-${idx}`;
                    span.textContent = word;
                    activeContainer.appendChild(span);
                });

                document.getElementById('line-prev').textContent = 
                    this.lyrics[(this.currentLine - 1 + this.lyrics.length) % this.lyrics.length].line;
                document.getElementById('line-next').textContent = 
                    this.lyrics[(this.currentLine + 1) % this.lyrics.length].line;
            }

            advanceWord() {
                const lineData = this.lyrics[this.currentLine];
                
                // Снять активность с предыдущего
                if (this.currentWord > 0) {
                    const prev = document.getElementById(`word-${this.currentWord - 1}`);
                    if (prev) prev.classList.remove('active');
                }

                if (this.currentWord < lineData.words.length) {
                    const curr = document.getElementById(`word-${this.currentWord}`);
                    if (curr) curr.classList.add('active');
                    this.playClickChome(320 + this.currentWord * 60);
                    this.currentWord++;
                } else {
                    this.currentWord = 0;
                    this.currentLine = (this.currentLine + 1) % this.lyrics.length;
                    this.renderLine();
                }
            }
        }

        const karaokeEngine = new AudioKaraokeEngine();

        // ==========================================
        // 2. 3D WEBGL СЦЕНА THREE.JS
        // ==========================================
        const container = document.getElementById('webgl-container');
        const scene = new THREE.Scene();
        scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x040306, 0.06);

        const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 100);
        camera.position.set(0, 0, 8.5);

        const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true });
        renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
        renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
        container.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

        // --- ЗОЛОТАЯ ЗВЕЗДНАЯ ПЫЛЬ В ВОЗДУХЕ ---
        const particleCount = 200;
        const particleGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const particlePositions = new Float32Array(particleCount * 3);

        for(let i = 0; i < particleCount * 3; i += 3) {
            particlePositions[i] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 16;
            particlePositions[i+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 10;
            particlePositions[i+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 10;
        }
        particleGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(particlePositions, 3));
        const particleMat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({
            color: 0xffd700,
            size: 0.04,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.6
        });
        const particles = new THREE.Points(particleGeo, particleMat);
        scene.add(particles);

        // --- ОВЕЩЕНИЕ ---
        const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.7);
        scene.add(ambientLight);

        const goldPointLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xffd700, 2.5, 12);
        goldPointLight.position.set(0, 3, 3);
        scene.add(goldPointLight);

        const bronzeLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xcd7f32, 3, 10);
        bronzeLight.position.set(-5, -2, 2);
        scene.add(bronzeLight);

        const crystalCyanLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x80daeb, 2, 10);
        crystalCyanLight.position.set(5, 2, -2);
        scene.add(crystalCyanLight);

        // --- ГЕНЕРАЦИЯ ТЕКСТУРЫ ГРАНЕЙ 4x4 (16 КНОПОК) ---
        const buttonLabels = [
            "NAV ↖", "TONE", "PITCH", "NAV ↗",
            "REVERB", "TEMPO", "MIC GAIN", "ECHO",
            "EQ HIGH", "EQ LOW", "HARMONY", "VOCAL FX",
            "NAV ↙", "PRESET", "SYNC", "NAV ↘"
        ];

        function createCubeTexture() {
            const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
            canvas.width = 512;
            canvas.height = 512;
            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10, 8, 14, 0.9)';
            ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 512, 512);

            ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffd700';
            ctx.lineWidth = 10;
            ctx.strokeRect(5, 5, 502, 502);

            const cellSize = 110;
            const padding = 12;
            const startX = 17;
            const startY = 17;

            for (let idx = 0; idx < 16; idx++) {
                const row = Math.floor(idx / 4);
                const col = idx % 4;
                const x = startX + col * (cellSize + padding);
                const y = startY + row * (cellSize + padding);

                const isNav = (row === 0 && col === 0) || (row === 0 && col === 3) || 
                              (row === 3 && col === 0) || (row === 3 && col === 3);

                // Градиент имперской бронзы и золота
                const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y, x + cellSize, y + cellSize);
                if (isNav) {
                    grad.addColorStop(0, '#ffd700');
                    grad.addColorStop(0.5, '#b8860b');
                    grad.addColorStop(1, '#cd7f32');
                } else {
                    grad.addColorStop(0, '#8c6239');
                    grad.addColorStop(0.5, '#4a3b32');
                    grad.addColorStop(1, '#231813');
                }

                ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.roundRect(x, y, cellSize, cellSize, 12);
                ctx.fill();

                ctx.strokeStyle = isNav ? '#ffffff' : '#ffd700';
                ctx.lineWidth = 3;
                ctx.stroke();

                ctx.fillStyle = isNav ? '#000000' : '#ffd700';
                ctx.font = 'bold 16px Arial';
                ctx.textAlign = 'center';
                ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
                ctx.fillText(buttonLabels[idx], x + cellSize / 2, y + cellSize / 2);
            }

            return new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
        }

        const faceTexture = createCubeTexture();

        // --- МАТЕРИАЛ ГОРНОГО ХРУСТАЛЯ С МАТРИЦЕЙ ПУЛЬТА ---
        const crystalMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
            color: 0xffffff,
            metalness: 0.1,
            roughness: 0.05,
            transmission: 0.9,
            thickness: 1.4,
            ior: 1.544, // Оптический коэффициент горного хрусталя
            reflectivity: 0.95,
            map: faceTexture,
            clearcoat: 1.0,
            clearcoatRoughness: 0.05,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.96
        });

        // --- ГЕОМЕТРИЯ КУБОВ И МОНТАЖ НА ВЕРШИНУ ---
        const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.65, 1.65, 1.65);

        const cubeLeft = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, crystalMaterial);
        const cubeRight = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, crystalMaterial);

        // Функция ориентации куба на нижнюю вершину
        function alignToVertex(mesh) {
            const diagVector = new THREE.Vector3(1, 1, 1).normalize();
            const upVector = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0);
            const quaternion = new THREE.Quaternion();
            quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(diagVector, upVector);
            mesh.rotation.setFromQuaternion(quaternion);
        }

        alignToVertex(cubeLeft);
        alignToVertex(cubeRight);

        // Поворотные шарниры (Pivots) для независимого вращения вокруг оси Y
        const pivotLeft = new THREE.Group();
        const pivotRight = new THREE.Group();

        pivotLeft.position.set(-3.3, 0, 0);
        pivotRight.position.set(3.3, 0, 0);

        scene.add(pivotLeft);
        scene.add(pivotRight);

        pivotLeft.add(cubeLeft);
        pivotRight.add(cubeRight);

        // --- ФИЗИКА ВРАЩЕНИЯ И ИМПУЛЬСЫ ---
        let speedLeft = 0;   // Левый куб: против часовой (+Y)
        let speedRight = 0;  // Правый куб: по часовой (-Y)
        const friction = 0.985;

        function updateFeedback(text) {
            const fb = document.getElementById('sys-feedback');
            fb.textContent = text;
            fb.style.color = '#ffd700';
            setTimeout(() => { fb.style.color = '#cd7f32'; }, 300);
        }

        // --- РЕАКЦИЯ НА 4 УГЛОВЫХ КВАДРАТА-ТОЛЧКА ---
        document.getElementById('btn-top-left').addEventListener('click', () => {
            speedLeft += 0.06;
            speedRight += 0.06;
            karaokeEngine.startKaraokeSequence();
            karaokeEngine.playClickChome(520);
            updateFeedback("ИМПУЛЬС: ТОЛЧОК ВЛЕВО (←)");
        });

        document.getElementById('btn-top-right').addEventListener('click', () => {
            speedLeft += 0.08;
            speedRight += 0.08;
            karaokeEngine.startKaraokeSequence();
            karaokeEngine.playClickChome(680);
            updateFeedback("ИМПУЛЬС: УСКОРЕНИЕ ВВЕРХ (↑)");
        });

        document.getElementById('btn-bottom-left').addEventListener('click', () => {
            speedLeft *= 0.2;
            speedRight *= 0.2;
            karaokeEngine.playClickChome(280);
            updateFeedback("ТОРМОЖЕНИЕ: ИМПУЛЬС ВНИЗ (↓)");
        });

        document.getElementById('btn-bottom-right').addEventListener('click', () => {
            speedLeft -= 0.06;
            speedRight -= 0.06;
            karaokeEngine.startKaraokeSequence();
            karaokeEngine.playClickChome(440);
            updateFeedback("ИМПУЛЬС: ТОЛЧОК ВПРАВО (→)");
        });

        // ==========================================
        // 3. 3D RAYCASTING И ИНТЕРАКТИВНОСТЬ КНОПОК 4x4
        // ==========================================
        const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
        const mouse = new THREE.Vector2();

        window.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
            mouse.x = (event.clientX / window.innerWidth) * 2 - 1;
            mouse.y = -(event.clientY / window.innerHeight) * 2 + 1;

            raycaster.setFromCamera(mouse, camera);
            const intersects = raycaster.intersectObject(cubeRight);

            if (intersects.length > 0) {
                const uv = intersects[0].uv;
                if (uv) {
                    const col = Math.floor(uv.x * 4);
                    const row = Math.floor((1 - uv.y) * 4);
                    const btnIndex = Math.min(15, Math.max(0, row * 4 + col));
                    const btnName = buttonLabels[btnIndex];

                    karaokeEngine.playClickChome(400 + btnIndex * 35);
                    updateFeedback(`ПУЛЬТ 4x4: [${btnName}] АКТИВИРОВАН`);

                    // Небольшой толчок при нажатии на грань
                    speedRight -= 0.03;
                }
            }
        });

        // ==========================================
        // 4. ГЛАВНЫЙ АНИМАЦИОННЫЙ ЦИКЛ
        // ==========================================
        const clock = new THREE.Clock();

        function animate() {
            requestAnimationFrame(animate);

            // Применение трения воздуха
            speedLeft *= friction;
            speedRight *= friction;

            // Базовое непрерывное вращение после первого толчка
            // Левый: против часовой стрелки (+Y)
            pivotLeft.rotation.y += (0.0015 + speedLeft);
            // Правый: по часовой стрелке (-Y)
            pivotRight.rotation.y -= (0.0015 + speedRight);

            // Легкое кинетическое парение в воздухе
            const elapsedTime = clock.getElapsedTime();
            pivotLeft.position.y = Math.sin(elapsedTime * 1.8) * 0.08;
            pivotRight.position.y = Math.cos(elapsedTime * 1.8) * 0.08;

            // Медленное вращение звездной пыли
            particles.rotation.y = elapsedTime * 0.03;

            renderer.render(scene, camera);
        }

        animate();

        // Адаптация под размер окна
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
            camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
            camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
            renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>
НЕТ ЧТОБ ПРИ АКТИВАЦИИ ОНИ ПОВОРАЧИВАЛИСЬ ТОЛЬКО НА СЛЕДУЮЩУЮ ГРАНЬ А САМИ КУБЫ БЫЛИ ИЗ ГОРНОГО ХРУСТАЛАЯ
We need to modify the code so that:

When activating (clicking corner triggers or 4x4 buttons), the cubes rotate exactly 90 degrees (one face) instead of continuous spinning with momentum.

The cubes should look like rock crystal (mountain crystal) - clear, transparent, with visible internal facets/reflections. The current material uses transmission but maybe we need a more pronounced crystal look, maybe with a wireframe or inner geometry.

Also, the user says "НЕТ ЧТОБ ПРИ АКТИВАЦИИ ОНИ ПОВОРАЧИВАЛИСЬ ТОЛЬКО НА СЛЕДУЮЩУЮ ГРАНЬ" - so instead of continuous rotation with momentum, each activation rotates exactly one face (90 degrees). "А САМИ КУБЫ БЫЛИ ИЗ ГОРНОГО ХРУСТАЛАЯ" - and the cubes themselves should be made of rock crystal.

So we need to:

Replace the physics-based rotation with a step-based rotation: each click adds a target rotation of 90 degrees, and animate smoothly to that target.

Ensure the rotation is around the correct axis (Y axis probably) and the cube is oriented with a vertex down, but rotating 90 degrees around Y will bring a new face forward. The 4x4 button matrix is on the right cube; we want each 90-degree rotation to present a new face? Actually the cube has 6 faces, but the texture is applied to all faces? The current createCubeTexture creates a 512x512 texture that is applied to all 6 faces (map: faceTexture). That means each face shows the same 4x4 grid. When rotating 90 degrees, you see the same grid. That's fine, but the buttons might not change. The user didn't ask to change the buttons per face, just that the cube rotates one face per activation.

But wait: the user says "ПРИ АКТИВАЦИИ ОНИ ПОВОРАЧИВАЛИСЬ ТОЛЬКО НА СЛЕДУЮЩУЮ ГРАНЬ" - so each activation rotates the cube by 90 degrees so a new face comes into view. That implies the cube might have different content on each face? Not necessarily; just the rotation behavior.

We also need to make the cubes look like rock crystal. The current material is MeshPhysicalMaterial with transmission, but maybe we can enhance it with more crystal-like properties: higher transmission, lower roughness, maybe add a subtle inner glow or facets. Also, we can add a wireframe or edges to emphasize the crystal structure. Perhaps we can add a second mesh with wireframe or a glowing edge.

Let's modify the texture function:

js
function createCubeTexture() {
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = 512;
    canvas.height = 512;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

    // Прозрачный фон
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, 512, 512);

    // Внешняя золотая рамка
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.8)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 10;
    ctx.strokeRect(5, 5, 502, 502);

    const cellSize = 110;
    const padding = 12;
    const startX = 17;
    const startY = 17;

    for (let idx = 0; idx < 16; idx++) {
        const row = Math.floor(idx / 4);
        const col = idx % 4;
        const x = startX + col * (cellSize + padding);
        const y = startY + row * (cellSize + padding);

        const isNav = (row === 0 && col === 0) || (row === 0 && col === 3) || 
                      (row === 3 && col === 0) || (row === 3 && col === 3);

        // Градиент с прозрачностью
        const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y, x + cellSize, y + cellSize);
        if (isNav) {
            grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.9)');
            grad.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(184, 134, 11, 0.9)');
            grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(205, 127, 50, 0.9)');
        } else {
            grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(140, 98, 57, 0.8)');
            grad.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(74, 59, 50, 0.8)');
            grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(35, 24, 19, 0.8)');
        }

        ctx.fillStyle = grad;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.roundRect(x, y, cellSize, cellSize, 12);
        ctx.fill();

        ctx.strokeStyle = isNav ? 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9)' : 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.8)';
        ctx.lineWidth = 3;
        ctx.stroke();

        ctx.fillStyle = isNav ? 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9)' : 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.9)';
        ctx.font = 'bold 16px Arial';
        ctx.textAlign = 'center';
        ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
        ctx.fillText(buttonLabels[idx], x + cellSize / 2, y + cellSize / 2);
    }

    return new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
}
This will make the background transparent, so the crystal material shows through. The buttons will be semi-transparent, giving an embedded look.

    targetRotRight -= step;

    // ... raycasting ...
    if (intersects.length > 0) {
        const uv = intersects[0].uv;
        if (uv) {
            // ... compute btnIndex ...
            karaokeEngine.playClickChome(400 + btnIndex * 35);
            updateFeedback(`ПУЛЬТ 4x4: [${btnName}] АКТИВИРОВАН`);

In animate:

js
function animate() {
    requestAnimationFrame(animate);

    // Плавный переход к целевым углам
    pivotLeft.rotation.y += (targetRotLeft - pivotLeft.rotation.y) * 0.1;
    pivotRight.rotation.y += (targetRotRight - pivotRight.rotation.y) * 0.1;

    // Легкое кинетическое парение в воздухе
    const elapsedTime = clock.getElapsedTime();
    pivotLeft.position.y = Math.sin(elapsedTime * 1.8) * 0.08;
    pivotRight.position.y = Math.cos(elapsedTime * 1.8) * 0.08;

    // Медленное вращение звездной пыли
    particles.rotation.y = elapsedTime * 0.03;

    renderer.render(scene, camera);
}
Remove speedLeft, speedRight, friction.

Also, the initial rotation of pivots is 0. That's fine.

We need to ensure that the 4x4 click doesn't also trigger the window click for corner triggers? Corner triggers are separate elements with their own click listeners. The window click listener will fire for any click, including on corner triggers. But in the original code, the window click listener only checks intersection with cubeRight. If the click is on a corner trigger, it won't intersect cubeRight (since it's an HTML element overlay), so it's fine. However, we should be careful: the corner trigger click event might propagate to window, but the raycast will not hit the cube because the mouse coordinates will be over the corner trigger, which is not part of the 3D canvas? Actually, the 3D canvas is behind, but the corner trigger is on top. The raycast uses the mouse coordinates relative to the window. If the click is on the corner trigger, the mouse coordinates are still over the 3D canvas area (since corner trigger is positioned over the canvas). So the raycast might still hit the cube behind the corner trigger. In the original code, this could cause the 4x4 button to activate when clicking a corner trigger if the corner trigger is over the cube. To prevent this, we can add a check in the window click listener: if the event target is a corner trigger or the karaoke stage, skip. Or we can stop propagation on corner triggers. Let's add event.stopPropagation() in the corner trigger listeners. That's cleaner.

So in corner trigger click handlers, add event.stopPropagation(). But the listeners currently don't receive the event object. We'll change to (event) => { ... event.stopPropagation(); ... }.

Also, for the 4x4 click, we might want to ignore clicks on the karaoke stage (which has pointer-events: none, so it won't capture clicks anyway). So fine.

        <div class="system-feedback" id="sys-feedback">СИСТЕМА ГОТОВА</div>
    </div>

    <div class="hint-overlay">КЛИКАЙТЕ ПО ГРАНЯМ ПРАВОГО КУБА-ПУЛЬТА ИЛИ УГЛОВЫМ КВАДРАТАМ</div>

    <script>
        // ==========================================
        // 1. СИНТЕЗАТОР ЗВУКА & KARAOKE ENGINE (Web Audio API)
        // ==========================================
        class AudioKaraokeEngine {
            constructor() {
                this.ctx = null;
                this.isPlaying = false;
                this.currentLine = 0;
                this.currentWord = 0;
                this.timer = null;

                this.lyrics = [
                    { line: "Хрустальный куб стоит на острой грани", words: ["Хрустальный", "куб", "стоит", "на", "острой", "грани"] },
                    { line: "Бронза и золото сияют в квантовом тумане", words: ["Бронза", "и", "золото", "сияют", "в", "квантовом", "тумане"] },
                    { line: "Вращение плавно пробуждает звук", words: ["Вращение", "плавно", "пробуждает", "звук"] },
                    { line: "Симфония импульса и магия рук", words: ["Симфония", "импульса", "и", "магия", "рук"] }
                ];
            }

            init() {
                if (!this.ctx) {
                    const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
                    this.ctx = new AudioContext();
                }
            }

            playClickChome(freq = 440) {
                this.init();
                if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume();

                const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                const gain = this.ctx.createGain();

                osc.type = 'sine';
                osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, this.ctx.currentTime);
                osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(freq * 1.8, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.15);

                gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.2, this.ctx.currentTime);
                gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.15);

                osc.connect(gain);
                gain.connect(this.ctx.destination);

                osc.start();
                osc.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 0.15);
            }

            startKaraokeSequence() {
                this.init();
                if (this.isPlaying) return;
                this.isPlaying = true;
                this.renderLine();

                this.timer = setInterval(() => {
                    this.advanceWord();
                }, 450);
            }

            renderLine() {
                const lineData = this.lyrics[this.currentLine];
                const activeContainer = document.getElementById('line-active');
                activeContainer.innerHTML = '';

                lineData.words.forEach((word, idx) => {
                    const span = document.createElement('span');
                    span.className = 'karaoke-word';
                    span.id = `word-${idx}`;
                    span.textContent = word;
                    activeContainer.appendChild(span);
                });

                document.getElementById('line-prev').textContent = 
                    this.lyrics[(this.currentLine - 1 + this.lyrics.length) % this.lyrics.length].line;
                document.getElementById('line-next').textContent = 
                    this.lyrics[(this.currentLine + 1) % this.lyrics.length].line;
            }

            advanceWord() {
                const lineData = this.lyrics[this.currentLine];
                
                if (this.currentWord > 0) {
                    const prev = document.getElementById(`word-${this.currentWord - 1}`);
                    if (prev) prev.classList.remove('active');
                }

                if (this.currentWord < lineData.words.length) {
                    const curr = document.getElementById(`word-${this.currentWord}`);
                    if (curr) curr.classList.add('active');
                    this.playClickChome(320 + this.currentWord * 60);
                    this.currentWord++;
                } else {
                    this.currentWord = 0;
                    this.currentLine = (this.currentLine + 1) % this.lyrics.length;
                    this.renderLine();
                }
            }
        }

        const karaokeEngine = new AudioKaraokeEngine();

        // ==========================================
        // 2. 3D WEBGL СЦЕНА THREE.JS
        // ==========================================
        const container = document.getElementById('webgl-container');
        const scene = new THREE.Scene();
        scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x040306, 0.06);

        const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 100);
        camera.position.set(0, 0, 8.5);

        const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true });
        renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
        renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
        container.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

        // --- ЗОЛОТАЯ ЗВЕЗДНАЯ ПЫЛЬ В ВОЗДУХЕ ---
        const particleCount = 200;
        const particleGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const particlePositions = new Float32Array(particleCount * 3);

        for(let i = 0; i < particleCount * 3; i += 3) {
            particlePositions[i] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 16;
            particlePositions[i+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 10;
            particlePositions[i+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 10;
        }
        particleGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(particlePositions, 3));
        const particleMat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({
            color: 0xffd700,
            size: 0.04,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.6
        });
        const particles = new THREE.Points(particleGeo, particleMat);
        scene.add(particles);

        // --- ОВЕЩЕНИЕ ---
        const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.7);
        scene.add(ambientLight);

        const goldPointLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xffd700, 2.5, 12);
        goldPointLight.position.set(0, 3, 3);
        scene.add(goldPointLight);

        const bronzeLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xcd7f32, 3, 10);
        bronzeLight.position.set(-5, -2, 2);
        scene.add(bronzeLight);

        const crystalCyanLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x80daeb, 2, 10);
        crystalCyanLight.position.set(5, 2, -2);
        scene.add(crystalCyanLight);

        // --- ГЕНЕРАЦИЯ ТЕКСТУРЫ ГРАНЕЙ 4x4 (16 КНОПОК) ---
        const buttonLabels = [
            "NAV ↖", "TONE", "PITCH", "NAV ↗",
            "REVERB", "TEMPO", "MIC GAIN", "ECHO",
            "EQ HIGH", "EQ LOW", "HARMONY", "VOCAL FX",
            "NAV ↙", "PRESET", "SYNC", "NAV ↘"
        ];

        function createCubeTexture() {
            const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
            canvas.width = 512;
            canvas.height = 512;
            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

            // Прозрачный фон – кристалл просвечивает
            ctx.clearRect(0, 0, 512, 512);

            // Внешняя золотая рамка
            ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.8)';
            ctx.lineWidth = 10;
            ctx.strokeRect(5, 5, 502, 502);

            const cellSize = 110;
            const padding = 12;
            const startX = 17;
            const startY = 17;

            for (let idx = 0; idx < 16; idx++) {
                const row = Math.floor(idx / 4);
                const col = idx % 4;
                const x = startX + col * (cellSize + padding);
                const y = startY + row * (cellSize + padding);

                const isNav = (row === 0 && col === 0) || (row === 0 && col === 3) || 
                              (row === 3 && col === 0) || (row === 3 && col === 3);

                const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y, x + cellSize, y + cellSize);
                if (isNav) {
                    grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.9)');
                    grad.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(184, 134, 11, 0.9)');
                    grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(205, 127, 50, 0.9)');
                } else {
                    grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(140, 98, 57, 0.8)');
                    grad.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(74, 59, 50, 0.8)');
                    grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(35, 24, 19, 0.8)');
                }

                ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.roundRect(x, y, cellSize, cellSize, 12);
                ctx.fill();

                ctx.strokeStyle = isNav ? 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9)' : 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.8)';
                ctx.lineWidth = 3;
                ctx.stroke();

                ctx.fillStyle = isNav ? 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9)' : 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.9)';
                ctx.font = 'bold 16px Arial';
                ctx.textAlign = 'center';
                ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
                ctx.fillText(buttonLabels[idx], x + cellSize / 2, y + cellSize / 2);
            }

            return new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
        }

        const faceTexture = createCubeTexture();

        // --- МАТЕРИАЛ ГОРНОГО ХРУСТАЛЯ ---
        const crystalMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
            color: 0xffffff,
            metalness: 0.0,
            roughness: 0.02,
            transmission: 1.0,
            thickness: 2.0,
            ior: 1.544, // Горный хрусталь
            reflectivity: 0.95,
            map: faceTexture,
            clearcoat: 1.0,
            clearcoatRoughness: 0.02,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.95,
            side: THREE.DoubleSide
        });

        // --- ГЕОМЕТРИЯ КУБОВ И МОНТАЖ НА ВЕРШИНУ ---
        const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.65, 1.65, 1.65);

        const cubeLeft = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, crystalMaterial);
        const cubeRight = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, crystalMaterial);

        // Добавляем светящиеся рёбра для кристаллического вида
        const edges = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(geometry);
        const lineMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffd700, transparent: true, opacity: 0.25 });
        const wireframeLeft = new THREE.LineSegments(edges, lineMat);
        const wireframeRight = new THREE.LineSegments(edges, lineMat);
        cubeLeft.add(wireframeLeft);
        cubeRight.add(wireframeRight);

        // Функция ориентации куба на нижнюю вершину
        function alignToVertex(mesh) {
            const diagVector = new THREE.Vector3(1, 1, 1).normalize();
            const upVector = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0);
            const quaternion = new THREE.Quaternion();
            quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(diagVector, upVector);
            mesh.rotation.setFromQuaternion(quaternion);
        }

        alignToVertex(cubeLeft);
        alignToVertex(cubeRight);

        // Поворотные шарниры (Pivots) для независимого вращения вокруг оси Y
        const pivotLeft = new THREE.Group();
        const pivotRight = new THREE.Group();

        pivotLeft.position.set(-3.3, 0, 0);
        pivotRight.position.set(3.3, 0, 0);

        scene.add(pivotLeft);
        scene.add(pivotRight);

        pivotLeft.add(cubeLeft);
        pivotRight.add(cubeRight);

        // --- ШАГОВОЕ ВРАЩЕНИЕ (ПО 90 ГРАДУСОВ) ---
        let targetRotLeft = 0;
        let targetRotRight = 0;
        const step = Math.PI / 2; // 90 градусов

        function updateFeedback(text) {
            const fb = document.getElementById('sys-feedback');
            fb.textContent = text;
            fb.style.color = '#ffd700';
            setTimeout(() => { fb.style.color = '#cd7f32'; }, 300);
        }

        // --- РЕАКЦИЯ НА 4 УГЛОВЫХ КВАДРАТА-ТОЛЧКА ---
        document.getElementById('btn-top-left').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            targetRotLeft += step;
            targetRotRight -= step;
            karaokeEngine.startKaraokeSequence();
            karaokeEngine.playClickChome(520);
            updateFeedback("ПОВОРОТ: СЛЕДУЮЩАЯ ГРАНЬ (←)");
        });

        document.getElementById('btn-top-right').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            targetRotLeft += step;
            targetRotRight -= step;
            karaokeEngine.startKaraokeSequence();
            karaokeEngine.playClickChome(680);
            updateFeedback("ПОВОРОТ: СЛЕДУЮЩАЯ ГРАНЬ (↑)");
        });

        document.getElementById('btn-bottom-left').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            targetRotLeft -= step;
            targetRotRight += step;
            karaokeEngine.playClickChome(280);
            updateFeedback("ПОВОРОТ: ПРЕДЫДУЩАЯ ГРАНЬ (↓)");
        });

        document.getElementById('btn-bottom-right').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            targetRotLeft += step;
            targetRotRight -= step;
            karaokeEngine.startKaraokeSequence();
            karaokeEngine.playClickChome(440);
            updateFeedback("ПОВОРОТ: СЛЕДУЮЩАЯ ГРАНЬ (→)");
        });

        // ==========================================
        // 3. 3D RAYCASTING И ИНТЕРАКТИВНОСТЬ КНОПОК 4x4
        // ==========================================
        const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
        const mouse = new THREE.Vector2();

        window.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
            mouse.x = (event.clientX / window.innerWidth) * 2 - 1;
            mouse.y = -(event.clientY / window.innerHeight) * 2 + 1;

            raycaster.setFromCamera(mouse, camera);
            const intersects = raycaster.intersectObject(cubeRight);

            if (intersects.length > 0) {
                const uv = intersects[0].uv;
                if (uv) {
                    const col = Math.floor(uv.x * 4);
                    const row = Math.floor((1 - uv.y) * 4);
                    const btnIndex = Math.min(15, Math.max(0, row * 4 + col));
                    const btnName = buttonLabels[btnIndex];

                    karaokeEngine.playClickChome(400 + btnIndex * 35);
                    updateFeedback(`ПУЛЬТ 4x4: [${btnName}] АКТИВИРОВАН`);

                    // Поворот правого куба на следующую грань
                    targetRotRight -= step;
                }
            }
        });

        // ==========================================
        // 4. ГЛАВНЫЙ АНИМАЦИОННЫЙ ЦИКЛ
        // ==========================================
        const clock = new THREE.Clock();

        function animate() {
            requestAnimationFrame(animate);

            // Плавный переход к целевым углам (шаговое вращение)
            pivotLeft.rotation.y += (targetRotLeft - pivotLeft.rotation.y) * 0.1;
            pivotRight.rotation.y += (targetRotRight - pivotRight.rotation.y) * 0.1;

            // Легкое кинетическое парение в воздухе
            const elapsedTime = clock.getElapsedTime();
            pivotLeft.position.y = Math.sin(elapsedTime * 1.8) * 0.08;
            pivotRight.position.y = Math.cos(elapsedTime * 1.8) * 0.08;

            // Медленное вращение звездной пыли
            particles.rotation.y = elapsedTime * 0.03;

            renderer.render(scene, camera);
        }

        animate();

        // Адаптация под размер окна
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
            camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
            camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
            renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        });
    </script>
</body>

function rotateLeft(clockwise) {
    // clockwise = true: rotate right cube -90, left cube +90 (or whatever)
    targetRotRight.value -= Math.PI/2;
    targetRotLeft.value += Math.PI/2;
}

Simpler: draw a chevron using two lines.

js
function drawChevron(ctx, cx, cy, size, angle) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(cx, cy);
    ctx.rotate(angle);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(-size/2, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(0, -size/2);
    ctx.lineTo(size/2, 0);
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffd700';
    ctx.lineWidth = size / 4;
    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
}
This draws a chevron pointing up when angle=0. To point different directions:

Up-left: angle = -Math.PI/4

Up-right: angle = Math.PI/4

Down-left: angle = -3*Math.PI/4

Down-right: angle = 3*Math.PI/4

Wait, chevron points up when angle=0, and rotating by angle rotates it. Let me think.

Chevron pointing up (^): angle 0

Points to up-left: rotate -45° = -PI/4

Points to up-right: rotate +45° = PI/4

Points down-left: rotate -135° = -3PI/4

Points down-right: rotate +135° = 3PI/4

Wait, one more thing. When user clicks a NAV button, we rotate. But the overlay is a child of the pivot, so it rotates too. Good.

For raycasting the overlay, I need to use raycaster.intersectObject(overlayLeft) and raycaster.intersectObject(overlayRight).

The UV coordinates will be from the overlay geometry, which is a box. UV.x and UV.y tell us the position on the clicked face.

Now, we need to know which face was clicked to know if it's a NAV cell. Actually no - the NAV cells are at the corners of any face's texture. So regardless of which face, the UV determines whether it's a NAV cell:

UV.x < 0.25 AND UV.y > 0.75 → top-left cell (NAV ↖)

UV.x > 0.75 AND UV.y > 0.75 → top-right cell (NAV ↗)

UV.x < 0.25 AND UV.y < 0.25 → bottom-left cell (NAV ↙)

UV.x > 0.75 AND UV.y < 0.25 → bottom-right cell (NAV ↘)

Wait, UV.y in three.js is typically 0 at bottom, 1 at top. But the texture is drawn with 0,0 at top-left. Actually for BoxGeometry UVs, v=0 is bottom and v=1 is top.

In the original code: const row = Math.floor((1 - uv.y) * 4); - this converts UV.y (bottom=0, top=1) to row (0=top, 3=bottom).

So UV.y close to 1 (top of face) → row 0. UV.y close to 0 (bottom) → row 3.

For top-left NAV: row 0, col 0 → UV.y > 0.75, UV.x < 0.25
For top-right NAV: row 0, col 3 → UV.y > 0.75, UV.x > 0.75
For bottom-left NAV: row 3, col 0 → UV.y < 0.25, UV.x < 0.25
For bottom-right NAV: row 3, col 3 → UV.y < 0.25, UV.x > 0.75

Good.

Let me write the texture function:

js
function createCubeTexture() {
    const size = 1024;
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = size;
    canvas.height = size;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    
    // Transparent background
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, size, size);
    
    // Border
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.9)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 16;
    ctx.strokeRect(8, 8, size - 16, size - 16);
    
    const cellSize = (size - 64) / 4;
    const padding = 8;
    const startX = 32;
    const startY = 32;
    
    for (let idx = 0; idx < 16; idx++) {
        const row = Math.floor(idx / 4);
        const col = idx % 4;
        const x = startX + col * (cellSize + padding);
        const y = startY + row * (cellSize + padding);
        
        const isNav = (row === 0 && col === 0) || (row === 0 && col === 3) ||
                      (row === 3 && col === 0) || (row === 3 && col === 3);
        
        // Cell background
        if (isNav) {
            const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(x + cellSize/2, y + cellSize/2, 0, x + cellSize/2, y + cellSize/2, cellSize);
            grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.7)');
            grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(184, 134, 11, 0.3)');
            ctx.fillStyle = grad;
        } else {
            const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y, x + cellSize, y + cellSize);
            grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(140, 98, 57, 0.5)');
            grad.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(74, 59, 50, 0.5)');
            grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(35, 24, 19, 0.5)');
            ctx.fillStyle = grad;
        }
        
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.roundRect(x, y, cellSize, cellSize, 20);
        ctx.fill();
        
        // Border
        ctx.strokeStyle = isNav ? 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9)' : 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.7)';
        ctx.lineWidth = 4;
        ctx.stroke();
        
        if (isNav) {
            // Draw arrow
            const cx = x + cellSize / 2;
            const cy = y + cellSize / 2;
            const arrowSize = cellSize * 0.5;
            
            let angle = 0;
            if (row === 0 && col === 0) angle = -Math.PI / 4;      // ↖
            else if (row === 0 && col === 3) angle = Math.PI / 4;  // ↗
            else if (row === 3 && col === 0) angle = -3 * Math.PI / 4; // ↙
            else if (row === 3 && col === 3) angle = 3 * Math.PI / 4;  // ↘
            
            ctx.save();
            ctx.translate(cx, cy);
            ctx.rotate(angle);
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
            ctx.lineWidth = arrowSize / 5;
            ctx.lineCap = 'round';
            ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(-arrowSize/3, arrowSize/3);
            ctx.lineTo(0, -arrowSize/3);
            ctx.lineTo(arrowSize/3, arrowSize/3);
            ctx.stroke();
            ctx.restore();
            
            // Label
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.85)';
            ctx.font = 'bold 28px Arial';
            ctx.textAlign = 'center';
            ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
            ctx.fillText('NAV', cx, cy + cellSize * 0.3);
        } else {
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.95)';
            ctx.font = 'bold 26px Arial';
            ctx.textAlign = 'center';
            ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
            ctx.fillText(buttonLabels[idx], x + cellSize / 2, y + cellSize / 2);
        }
    }
    
    const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
    texture.anisotropy = 16;
    return texture;
}

Actually, let me just create a simple gradient environment by using a scene with colored lights and rendering it:

js
function createEnvironment() {
    const envScene = new THREE.Scene();
    envScene.background = new THREE.Color(0x0a0614);
    
    // Add emissive planes to act as "studio lights" for reflections
    const geo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(20, 20);
    
    const colors = [0xffd700, 0xcd7f32, 0x80daeb, 0xffffff];
    const positions = [
        [10, 10, 10], [-10, 5, 10], [10, -5, -10], [0, 15, 0]
    ];
    
    colors.forEach((c, i) => {
        const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: c, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
        mesh.position.set(...positions[i]);
        mesh.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
        envScene.add(mesh);
    });
    
    return pmremGenerator.fromScene(envScene, 0.1).texture;
}
This creates an environment with colored planes that will reflect on the crystal.

For rotation handling:

js
let targetRotLeft = 0;
let targetRotRight = 0;
const STEP = Math.PI / 2;

function rotateCubes(direction) {
    // direction: +1 for "next" (clockwise), -1 for "previous"
    targetRotRight += direction * STEP;
    targetRotLeft -= direction * STEP;
}

Actually, the simplest thing: just create a gradient environment texture from a canvas and use it as the environment.

js
function createGradientEnv() {
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = 512;
    canvas.height = 512;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 512);
    grad.addColorStop(0, '#1a1a3a');
    grad.addColorStop(0.5, '#4a3b52');
    grad.addColorStop(1, '#0a0614');
    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 512, 512);
    // Add some bright spots
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.8)';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(128, 128, 60, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(128, 218, 235, 0.6)';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(384, 256, 80, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(205, 127, 50, 0.7)';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(256, 400, 70, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    
    const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
    texture.mapping = THREE.EquirectangularReflectionMapping;
    return texture;
}

scene.environment = createGradientEnv();

js
function getButtonIndex(uv) {
    const col = Math.min(3, Math.floor(uv.x * 4));
    const row = Math.min(3, Math.floor((1 - uv.y) * 4));
    return row * 4 + col;
}

function isNavButton(idx) {
    return idx === 0 || idx === 3 || idx === 12 || idx === 15;
}

function getNavDirection(idx) {
    // returns -1 (rotate left) or +1 (rotate right) or 0 (not nav)

Let me think again carefully. If I'm looking at the front face of a cube, and I want to see the face that's currently on the LEFT side of the cube:

I need to rotate the cube so that -X becomes +Z (facing me)

Rotating around Y by +90°: local +Z → world +X, so local -X → world +Z. Yes! Rotating +90° brings the left face to the front.

Wait let me redo. Rotation matrix around Y by angle θ:

[cos θ, 0, sin θ]

[0, 1, 0]

[-sin θ, 0, cos θ]

Applying to vector (x, y, z):

new_x = x * cos θ + z * sin θ

new_z = -x * sin θ + z * cos θ

For θ = +90° (cos=0, sin=1):

(1,0,0) → (0, 0, -1) : +X goes to -Z (right face goes to back)

(0,0,1) → (1, 0, 0) : +Z goes to +X (front face goes to right)

(-1,0,0) → (0, 0, 1) : -X goes to +Z (left face goes to front!) ✓

So +90° rotation brings the LEFT face to the FRONT. Good.

So:

To see the left face: rotate +90°

To see the right face: rotate -90°

Therefore:

NAV ↖ / NAV ↙ (left side): +90°

NAV ↗ / NAV ↘ (right side): -90°

Actually wait, I realize there's a potential issue with using map on a transmissive material. Let me test my understanding:

In three.js r128, MeshPhysicalMaterial:

map affects the diffuse color of the material

When transmission > 0, the material becomes "transmissive" - light passes through

The transmissive appearance is computed separately from the base color

The base color (from map) is used for the "diffuse" part

With high transmission, the diffuse part is attenuated

So with transmission: 0.95, the map would only contribute ~5% of the appearance, which would be too faint.

Solutions:

Use emissiveMap - emissive is added on top regardless of transmission

Use map and lower transmission

Use a separate overlay mesh

Option 1 might work. Let me try:

js
emissive: 0xffffff,
emissiveMap: faceTexture,
emissiveIntensity: 0.6,
But then the transparent areas of the texture would still emit? No - emissiveMap only adds where the texture has color. Where the texture is transparent (RGBA = 0,0,0,0), the emissive contribution is 0. So it should be fine.

Wait, actually, does three.js support alpha in emissiveMap? Let me think... emissiveMap is multiplied by the emissive color to get the emissive contribution. The alpha channel of emissiveMap isn't used as a mask (I think). So where the texture is transparent but has RGB values of 0, the emissive is 0. That should work.

Actually, if I clearRect the canvas, the RGB values are 0,0,0 and alpha is 0. So emissive contribution is 0 in those areas. Good.

But wait, when I draw buttons with semi-transparent backgrounds, the canvas stores premultiplied alpha or straight alpha? Canvas 2D stores straight alpha (I think). So drawing with rgba(140, 98, 57, 0.5) means the pixel is (140, 98, 57, 0.5) - RGB is the actual color, alpha is 0.5. When used as emissiveMap, three.js would multiply emissive by (140/255, 98/255, 57/255), giving a dim brownish glow. That's actually a nice effect.

But the transparency (alpha 0.5) is not used by emissiveMap, so those pixels emit at full strength of (140,98,57)/255 even though they're semi-transparent in the map. Hmm.

OK let me not overthink. Let me try both approaches and see.

OK let me code:

js
function drawChevron(ctx, cx, cy, size, angle) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(cx, cy);
    ctx.rotate(angle);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(-size/2, size/2);
    ctx.lineTo(0, -size/2);
    ctx.lineTo(size/2, size/2);
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffd700';
    ctx.lineWidth = size * 0.2;
    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
}
This draws a chevron pointing up. Rotating by angle changes direction.

For ↖: angle = -PI/4 (rotate the up-chevron 45° counterclockwise)
For ↗: angle = PI/4
For ↙: angle = -3PI/4
For ↘: angle = 3PI/4

    reflectivity: 1.0,
    clearcoat: 1.0,
    clearcoatRoughness: 0.0,
    transparent: true,
    opacity: 0.85,
    side: THREE.FrontSide
});
And for buttons:

js
const buttonMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
    map: faceTexture,
    transparent: true,
    alphaTest: 0.5,
    metalness: 0.7,
    roughness: 0.3,
    emissive: 0x332200,
    emissiveIntensity: 0.5,
    side: THREE.FrontSide
});
Wait, using emissive with alphaTest - emissive will show on non-transparent areas. Actually the emissive color is just added, and where alpha < 0.5 the pixel is discarded anyway. So emissive only shows on buttons. Good. This gives a nice "backlit" look to the buttons.

OK let me write it.

Actually, let me reconsider using both transmission AND transparent. In Three.js:

transmission > 0 makes the material use the transmission render path

transparent: true and opacity < 1 make the material use alpha blending

Using both can cause issues. Let me just use transmission without transparent: true. Actually, transmission implies some level of transparency. Let me try:

js
const crystalMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
    color: 0xd8e8ff,
    metalness: 0.0,
    roughness: 0.02,
    transmission: 0.95,
    thickness: 2.5,
    ior: 1.544,
    reflectivity: 1.0,
    clearcoat: 1.0,
    clearcoatRoughness: 0.0
});
Without transparent: true. But transmission requires the material to be treated as transparent... let me check three.js docs.

Actually, from three.js docs: "transmission - Degree of transmission (or optical transparency), from 0.0 to 1.0. Default is 0. When transmission is 0, the material is opaque. Above 0, the material becomes transparent (you will also want to set transparent: true or use alphaTest)."

OK so we need transparent: true for transmission to work. Got it.

Actually, I've seen examples where transmission works without transparent: true, because transmission has its own render path. Let me just set both to be safe.

js
const crystalMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
    color: 0xd8e8ff,
    metalness: 0.0,
    roughness: 0.02,
    transmission: 0.95,
    thickness: 2.5,
    ior: 1.544,
    reflectivity: 1.0,
    clearcoat: 1.0,
    clearcoatRoughness: 0.0,
    transparent: true,
    opacity: 1.0  // Don't use opacity for transparency, use transmission
});
Hmm, if opacity: 1.0 and transparent: true, the material is technically not alpha-blended. But transmission handles the transparency. OK.

Alright, writing the code.

const pmremGenerator = new THREE.PMREMGenerator(renderer);

const envCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');

// Dark gradient background
const bgGrad = ectx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 512);
bgGrad.addColorStop(0, '#1a1530');
bgGrad.addColorStop(0.5, '#0a0614');
bgGrad.addColorStop(1, '#040306');
ectx.fillStyle = bgGrad;
ectx.fillRect(0, 0, 1024, 512);

// Bright spots for specular reflections
const spots = [
    { x: 200, y: 100, r: 80, color: 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.9)' },
    { x: 700, y: 150, r: 60, color: 'rgba(128, 218, 235, 0.8)' },
    { x: 500, y: 300, r: 100, color: 'rgba(205, 127, 50, 0.7)' },
    { x: 850, y: 400, r: 70, color: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.6)' }

spots.forEach(s => {

    g.addColorStop(0, s.color);
    g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)');
    ectx.fillStyle = g;
    ectx.beginPath();
    ectx.arc(s.x, s.y, s.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ectx.fill();
});

const envMap = pmremGenerator.fromEquirectangular(envTexture).texture;
scene.environment = envMap;
This should give nice reflections on the crystal.

    <!-- Центральный интерфейс Караоке -->
    <div class="karaoke-stage">

    <script>
        // ==========================================
        // 1. СИНТЕЗАТОР ЗВУКА & KARAOKE ENGINE (Web Audio API)
        // ==========================================
        class AudioKaraokeEngine {
            constructor() {
                this.ctx = null;
                this.isPlaying = false;
                this.currentLine = 0;
                this.currentWord = 0;
                this.timer = null;

                this.lyrics = [
                    { line: "Хрустальный куб стоит на острой грани", words: ["Хрустальный", "куб", "стоит", "на", "острой", "грани"] },
                    { line: "Бронза и золото сияют в квантовом тумане", words: ["Бронза", "и", "золото", "сияют", "в", "квантовом", "тумане"] },
                    { line: "Вращение плавно пробуждает звук", words: ["Вращение", "плавно", "пробуждает", "звук"] },
                    { line: "Симфония импульса и магия рук", words: ["Симфония", "импульса", "и", "магия", "рук"] }
                ];
            }

            init() {
                if (!this.ctx) {
                    const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
                    this.ctx = new AudioContext();
                }
            }

            playClickChome(freq = 440) {
                this.init();
                if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume();

                const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                const gain = this.ctx.createGain();

                osc.type = 'sine';
                osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, this.ctx.currentTime);
                osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(freq * 1.8, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.15);

                gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.2, this.ctx.currentTime);
                gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.15);

                osc.connect(gain);
                gain.connect(this.ctx.destination);

                osc.start();
                osc.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 0.15);
            }

            startKaraokeSequence() {
                this.init();
                if (this.isPlaying) return;
                this.isPlaying = true;
                this.renderLine();

                this.timer = setInterval(() => {
                    this.advanceWord();
                }, 450);
            }

            renderLine() {
                const lineData = this.lyrics[this.currentLine];
                const activeContainer = document.getElementById('line-active');
                activeContainer.innerHTML = '';

                lineData.words.forEach((word, idx) => {
                    const span = document.createElement('span');
                    span.className = 'karaoke-word';
                    span.id = `word-${idx}`;
                    span.textContent = word;
                    activeContainer.appendChild(span);
                });

                document.getElementById('line-prev').textContent =
                    this.lyrics[(this.currentLine - 1 + this.lyrics.length) % this.lyrics.length].line;
                document.getElementById('line-next').textContent =
                    this.lyrics[(this.currentLine + 1) % this.lyrics.length].line;
            }

            advanceWord() {
                const lineData = this.lyrics[this.currentLine];

                if (this.currentWord > 0) {
                    const prev = document.getElementById(`word-${this.currentWord - 1}`);
                    if (prev) prev.classList.remove('active');
                }

                if (this.currentWord < lineData.words.length) {
                    const curr = document.getElementById(`word-${this.currentWord}`);
                    if (curr) curr.classList.add('active');
                    this.playClickChome(320 + this.currentWord * 60);
                    this.currentWord++;
                } else {
                    this.currentWord = 0;
                    this.currentLine = (this.currentLine + 1) % this.lyrics.length;
                    this.renderLine();
                }
            }
        }

        const karaokeEngine = new AudioKaraokeEngine();

        // ==========================================
        // 2. 3D WEBGL СЦЕНА THREE.JS
        // ==========================================
        const container = document.getElementById('webgl-container');
        const scene = new THREE.Scene();
        scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x040306, 0.06);

        const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 100);
        camera.position.set(0, 0.4, 8.5);
        camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);

        const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true });
        renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
        renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
        renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.1;
        container.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

        // --- ОКРУЖЕНИЕ ДЛЯ ОТРАЖЕНИЙ (PMREM Environment) ---
        (function buildEnvironment() {
            const pmremGenerator = new THREE.PMREMGenerator(renderer);
            pmremGenerator.compileEquirectangularShader();

            const envCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
            envCanvas.width = 1024;
            envCanvas.height = 512;
            const ectx = envCanvas.getContext('2d');

            const bgGrad = ectx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 512);
            bgGrad.addColorStop(0, '#15102a');
            bgGrad.addColorStop(0.5, '#0a0614');
            bgGrad.addColorStop(1, '#040306');
            ectx.fillStyle = bgGrad;
            ectx.fillRect(0, 0, 1024, 512);

            const spots = [
                { x: 200, y: 100, r: 120, color: 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.95)' },
                { x: 700, y: 150, r: 90, color: 'rgba(128, 218, 235, 0.85)' },
                { x: 500, y: 320, r: 140, color: 'rgba(205, 127, 50, 0.8)' },
                { x: 850, y: 400, r: 100, color: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7)' }
            ];

            spots.forEach(s => {
                const g = ectx.createRadialGradient(s.x, s.y, 0, s.x, s.y, s.r);
                g.addColorStop(0, s.color);
                g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)');
                ectx.fillStyle = g;
                ectx.beginPath();
                ectx.arc(s.x, s.y, s.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
                ectx.fill();
            });

            const envTexture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(envCanvas);
            envTexture.mapping = THREE.EquirectangularReflectionMapping;

            const envMap = pmremGenerator.fromEquirectangular(envTexture).texture;
            scene.environment = envMap;

            envTexture.dispose();
            pmremGenerator.dispose();
        })();

        // --- ЗОЛОТАЯ ЗВЕЗДНАЯ ПЫЛЬ В ВОЗДУХЕ ---
        const particleCount = 260;
        const particleGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const particlePositions = new Float32Array(particleCount * 3);

        for (let i = 0; i < particleCount * 3; i += 3) {
            particlePositions[i] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 18;
            particlePositions[i + 1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 12;
            particlePositions[i + 2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 12;
        }
        particleGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(particlePositions, 3));
        const particleMat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({
            color: 0xffd700,
            size: 0.04,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.6,
            depthWrite: false
        });
        const particles = new THREE.Points(particleGeo, particleMat);
        scene.add(particles);

        // --- ОСВЕЩЕНИЕ ---
        const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.5);
        scene.add(ambientLight);

        const goldPointLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xffd700, 3.5, 14);
        goldPointLight.position.set(0, 4, 4);
        scene.add(goldPointLight);

        const bronzeLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xcd7f32, 4, 12);
        bronzeLight.position.set(-6, -2, 3);
        scene.add(bronzeLight);

        const crystalCyanLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x80daeb, 3, 12);
        crystalCyanLight.position.set(6, 2, -3);
        scene.add(crystalCyanLight);

        const keyLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.9);
        keyLight.position.set(2, 5, 6);
        scene.add(keyLight);

        // ==========================================
        // 3. ТЕКСТУРА ГРАНЕЙ 4x4 (NAV-стрелки в углах)
        // ==========================================
        const buttonLabels = [
            "NAV", "TONE", "PITCH", "NAV",
            "REVERB", "TEMPO", "MIC GAIN", "ECHO",
            "EQ HIGH", "EQ LOW", "HARMONY", "VOCAL FX",
            "NAV", "PRESET", "SYNC", "NAV"
        ];

        function drawChevron(ctx, cx, cy, size, angle) {
            ctx.save();
            ctx.translate(cx, cy);
            ctx.rotate(angle);
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(-size * 0.45, size * 0.4);
            ctx.lineTo(0, -size * 0.45);
            ctx.lineTo(size * 0.45, size * 0.4);
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
            ctx.lineWidth = size * 0.22;
            ctx.lineCap = 'round';
            ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
            ctx.stroke();
            ctx.restore();
        }

        function createCubeTexture() {
            const SIZE = 1024;
            const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
            canvas.width = SIZE;
            canvas.height = SIZE;
            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

            // Полностью прозрачный фон — кристалл просвечивает
            ctx.clearRect(0, 0, SIZE, SIZE);

            // Внешняя золотая рамка грани
            ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.85)';
            ctx.lineWidth = 14;
            ctx.strokeRect(10, 10, SIZE - 20, SIZE - 20);

            const gridPad = 40;
            const gap = 12;
            const cellSize = (SIZE - gridPad * 2 - gap * 3) / 4;

            for (let idx = 0; idx < 16; idx++) {
                const row = Math.floor(idx / 4);
                const col = idx % 4;
                const x = gridPad + col * (cellSize + gap);
                const y = gridPad + row * (cellSize + gap);

                const isNav = (row === 0 && col === 0) || (row === 0 && col === 3) ||
                              (row === 3 && col === 0) || (row === 3 && col === 3);

                const radius = cellSize * 0.18;

                // Фон кнопки
                const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y, x + cellSize, y + cellSize);
                if (isNav) {
                    grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.95)');
                    grad.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(184, 134, 11, 0.95)');
                    grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(205, 127, 50, 0.95)');
                } else {
                    grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(120, 82, 48, 0.92)');
                    grad.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(58, 45, 38, 0.92)');
                    grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(28, 18, 14, 0.92)');
                }

                ctx.fillStyle = grad;
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.roundRect(x, y, cellSize, cellSize, radius);
                ctx.fill();

                // Обводка
                ctx.strokeStyle = isNav ? 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95)' : 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.8)';
                ctx.lineWidth = isNav ? 6 : 3;
                ctx.beginPath();
                ctx.roundRect(x, y, cellSize, cellSize, radius);
                ctx.stroke();

                const cx = x + cellSize / 2;
                const cy = y + cellSize / 2;

                if (isNav) {
                    // Рисуем диагональную стрелку-шеврон
                    let angle = 0;
                    if (row === 0 && col === 0) angle = -Math.PI / 4;         // ↖
                    else if (row === 0 && col === 3) angle = Math.PI / 4;     // ↗
                    else if (row === 3 && col === 0) angle = -3 * Math.PI / 4;// ↙
                    else if (row === 3 && col === 3) angle = 3 * Math.PI / 4; // ↘

                    drawChevron(ctx, cx, cy - cellSize * 0.08, cellSize * 0.55, angle);

                    // Подпись NAV
                    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.85)';
                    ctx.font = `bold ${Math.floor(cellSize * 0.16)}px Arial`;
                    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
                    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
                    ctx.fillText('NAV', cx, y + cellSize * 0.86);
                } else {
                    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 215, 0, 0.95)';
                    ctx.font = `bold ${Math.floor(cellSize * 0.17)}px Arial`;
                    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
                    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
                    ctx.fillText(buttonLabels[idx], cx, cy);
                }
            }

            const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
            texture.anisotropy = 16;
            return texture;
        }

        const faceTexture = createCubeTexture();

        // ==========================================
        // 4. МАТЕРИАЛЫ ГОРНОГО ХРУСТАЛЯ И КНОПОК
        // ==========================================

        // Кристалл — прозрачный кварц с истинным IOR
        const crystalMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
            color: 0xdce8ff,
            metalness: 0.0,
            roughness: 0.02,
            transmission: 0.95,
            thickness: 2.5,
            ior: 1.544,               // горный хрусталь (кварц)
            reflectivity: 1.0,
            clearcoat: 1.0,
            clearcoatRoughness: 0.0,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.9,
            side: THREE.FrontSide,
            envMapIntensity: 1.8
        });

        // Матрица кнопок-накладок (накладывается поверх граней)
        const buttonMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            map: faceTexture,
            transparent: true,
            alphaTest: 0.5,
            metalness: 0.75,
            roughness: 0.28,
            emissive: 0x1a0e00,
            emissiveIntensity: 0.8,
            side: THREE.FrontSide,
            envMapIntensity: 1.2
        });

        // ==========================================
        // 5. ГЕОМЕТРИЯ КУБОВ, МОНТАЖ И ШАРНИРЫ
        // ==========================================
        const CUBE_SIZE = 1.65;
        const crystalGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(CUBE_SIZE, CUBE_SIZE, CUBE_SIZE);
        const buttonGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(CUBE_SIZE * 1.004, CUBE_SIZE * 1.004, CUBE_SIZE * 1.004);

        function alignToVertex(mesh) {
            const diagVector = new THREE.Vector3(1, 1, 1).normalize();
            const upVector = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0);
            const quaternion = new THREE.Quaternion();
            quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(diagVector, upVector);
            mesh.rotation.setFromQuaternion(quaternion);
        }

        // Левый куб
        const crystalLeft = new THREE.Mesh(crystalGeo, crystalMaterial);
        const buttonsLeft = new THREE.Mesh(buttonGeo, buttonMaterial);
        alignToVertex(crystalLeft);
        alignToVertex(buttonsLeft);

        // Правый куб
        const crystalRight = new THREE.Mesh(crystalGeo, crystalMaterial);
        const buttonsRight = new THREE.Mesh(buttonGeo, buttonMaterial);
        alignToVertex(crystalRight);
        alignToVertex(buttonsRight);

        // Шарниры вращения
        const pivotLeft = new THREE.Group();
        const pivotRight = new THREE.Group();

        pivotLeft.position.set(-3.3, 0, 0);
        pivotRight.position.set(3.3, 0, 0);

        pivotLeft.add(crystalLeft);
        pivotLeft.add(buttonsLeft);
        pivotRight.add(crystalRight);
        pivotRight.add(buttonsRight);

        scene.add(pivotLeft);
        scene.add(pivotRight);

        // ==========================================
        // 6. ШАГОВОЕ ВРАЩЕНИЕ (90° за клик)
        // ==========================================
        const STEP = Math.PI / 2;
        let targetRotLeft = 0;
        let targetRotRight = 0;

        function updateFeedback(text) {
            const fb = document.getElementById('sys-feedback');
            fb.textContent = text;
            fb.style.color = '#ffd700';
            clearTimeout(fb._t);
            fb._t = setTimeout(() => { fb.style.color = '#cd7f32'; }, 400);
        }

        // Направление: +1 = поворот вправо (видна правая грань), -1 = влево
        function rotateCubes(direction) {
            targetRotRight += direction * STEP;
            targetRotLeft -= direction * STEP;

            // Нормализация при больших углах
            const TWO_PI = Math.PI * 2;
            if (Math.abs(targetRotRight) > TWO_PI * 8) {
                const shiftR = Math.round(targetRotRight / TWO_PI) * TWO_PI;
                targetRotRight -= shiftR;
                pivotRight.rotation.y -= shiftR;
            }
            if (Math.abs(targetRotLeft) > TWO_PI * 8) {
                const shiftL = Math.round(targetRotLeft / TWO_PI) * TWO_PI;
                targetRotLeft -= shiftL;
                pivotLeft.rotation.y -= shiftL;
            }
        }

        // ==========================================
        // 7. RAYCASTING ПО ГРАНЯМ КУБОВ
        // ==========================================
        const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
        const mouse = new THREE.Vector2();

        // Индексы NAV-ячеек в матрице 4x4
        // 0 = ↖ (верх-лево), 3 = ↗ (верх-право),
        // 12 = ↙ (низ-лево), 15 = ↘ (низ-право)
        function handleButtonClick(uv) {
            const col = Math.min(3, Math.max(0, Math.floor(uv.x * 4)));
            const row = Math.min(3, Math.max(0, Math.floor((1 - uv.y) * 4)));
            const btnIndex = row * 4 + col;

            // Левые NAV → поворот влево (view moves left) → direction = -1
            // Правые NAV → поворот вправо → direction = +1
            if (btnIndex === 0 || btnIndex === 12) {
                rotateCubes(-1);
                karaokeEngine.playClickChome(520);
                karaokeEngine.startKaraokeSequence();
                updateFeedback('NAV ← : ПОВОРОТ НА ГРАНЬ ВЛЕВО');
            } else if (btnIndex === 3 || btnIndex === 15) {
                rotateCubes(+1);
                karaokeEngine.playClickChome(680);
                karaokeEngine.startKaraokeSequence();
                updateFeedback('NAV → : ПОВОРОТ НА ГРАНЬ ВПРАВО');
            } else {
                // Обычная кнопка пульта
                karaokeEngine.playClickChome(400 + btnIndex * 35);
                updateFeedback(`ПУЛЬТ: [${buttonLabels[btnIndex]}] АКТИВИРОВАН`);
            }
        }

        window.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
            mouse.x = (event.clientX / window.innerWidth) * 2 - 1;
            mouse.y = -(event.clientY / window.innerHeight) * 2 + 1;

            raycaster.setFromCamera(mouse, camera);

            // Проверяем обе накладки с кнопками
            const hitsR = raycaster.intersectObject(buttonsRight);
            const hitsL = raycaster.intersectObject(buttonsLeft);

            let hit = null;
            if (hitsR.length > 0) hit = hitsR[0];
            if (hitsL.length > 0 && (!hit || hitsL[0].distance < hit.distance)) {
                hit = hitsL[0];
            }

            if (hit && hit.uv) {
                handleButtonClick(hit.uv);
            }
        });

        // ==========================================
        // 8. ГЛАВНЫЙ АНИМАЦИОННЫЙ ЦИКЛ
        // ==========================================
        const clock = new THREE.Clock();

        function animate() {
            requestAnimationFrame(animate);

            // Плавная интерполяция к целевым углам (эффект шагового поворота)
            pivotRight.rotation.y += (targetRotRight - pivotRight.rotation.y) * 0.09;
            pivotLeft.rotation.y  += (targetRotLeft  - pivotLeft.rotation.y)  * 0.09;

            // Легкое кинетическое парение
            const t = clock.getElapsedTime();
            pivotLeft.position.y  = Math.sin(t * 1.8) * 0.08;
            pivotRight.position.y = Math.cos(t * 1.8) * 0.08;

            // Медленный дрейф звёздной пыли
            particles.rotation.y = t * 0.03;

            renderer.render(scene, camera);
        }

        animate();

        // Адаптация под размер окна
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
            camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
            camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
            renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

js
function createButtonGeometry(size, thickness, cornerRadius) {
    const shape = new THREE.Shape();
    const x = -size/2, y = -size/2, w = size, h = size, r = cornerRadius;
    shape.moveTo(x + r, y);
    shape.lineTo(x + w - r, y);
    shape.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + r);
    shape.lineTo(x + w, y + h - r);
    shape.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y + h, x + w - r, y + h);
    shape.lineTo(x + r, y + h);
    shape.quadraticCurveTo(x, y + h, x, y + h - r);
    shape.lineTo(x, y + r);
    shape.quadraticCurveTo(x, y, x + r, y);
    
    const extrudeSettings = {
        depth: thickness,
        bevelEnabled: true,
        bevelThickness: thickness * 0.3,
        bevelSize: size * 0.05,
        bevelSegments: 2
    };
    return new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(shape, extrudeSettings);
}
This creates a rounded box with chamfered edges - looks like a raised button.

js
function createConvexButtonGeometry(size, height) {
    const geo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(size, size, 12, 12);
    const pos = geo.attributes.position;
    const half = size / 2;
    for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
        const x = pos.getX(i) / half;
        const y = pos.getY(i) / half;
        const d = Math.sqrt(x*x + y*y);
        const z = Math.cos(d * Math.PI * 0.5) * height;
        pos.setZ(i, z);
    }
    geo.computeVertexNormals();
    return geo;
}
This creates a domed surface. The button is essentially a "bubble" - only the top surface. Then a thin box behind it or just rely on it.
